&lt;project_context>
# 项目: distill_sparse_swiglu

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: westd-34887
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh connect.westd.seetacloud.com  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/

## ⚠️ 活跃约束（必须遵守）

- **C001**: 只允许使用 westd-34887 的 cuda:0 和 cuda:1，禁止使用其他 GPU（原因：项目分配 GPU 为 RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell × 2，Reviewer 要求配置 GPU 边界以满足 L1 红线 #4） [来自 D001]


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格

&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：写 train_bce_comp.py 独立训练脚本

### 背景
项目路径：westd-34887 服务器 /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/
当前已有：
- `src/train.py` — BCE 和 KL normalized 训练（已通过 review）
- `src/losses.py` — CompensationLoss (MSE: sparse+comp vs dense), SparsityRegularizer, KLDistillLoss, BCEPredictorLoss（已 review PASS）
- `src/predictor.py` — PredictorWrapper, CompensationHead (hidden→256→hidden bottleneck), CompensationNetwork（已 review PASS）
- `src/model.py` — 模型加载和 MLP 结构
- `src/data_utils.py` — 数据加载
- 之前有 Worker 在 train.py 中添加了 --use_compensation flag 和 train_bce_comp() 函数，但 Director 要求写一个独立的 train_bce_comp.py

### 具体要求

1. **先 SSH 读取现有代码**（ssh_read_only）：
   - `cat /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/src/train.py` — 完整理解训练循环结构
   - `cat /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/src/losses.py` — 理解 CompensationLoss 和 SparsityRegularizer 接口
   - `cat /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/src/predictor.py` — 理解 CompensationHead/CompensationNetwork 接口
   - `cat /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/src/model.py` — 理解 MLP 结构

2. **写 src/train_bce_comp.py**：
   基于 train.py 改造，独立脚本，关键差异：

   **训练流程（两阶段）**：
   - Phase 1: BCE-only warmup（前 comp_warmup_steps 步）
     - 只训练 predictor，不训练 CompensationHead
     - 用 BCEPredictorLoss + SparsityRegularizer
   - Phase 2: BCE + Compensation 联合训练（warmup 后）
     - predictor 继续训练
     - 加入 CompensationHead，用 CompensationLoss (MSE: compensation(sparse_output) vs dense_output)
     - 总 loss = bce_loss + comp_loss_weight * comp_mse_loss + reg_weight * sparsity_reg

   **配置参数（CLI args）**：
   - --comp_warmup_steps (default=200): BCE-only warmup 步数
   - --comp_loss_weight (default=1.0): compensation loss 权重
   - --comp_bottleneck (default=256): CompensationHead bottleneck 维度
   - --predictor_lr (default=1e-3): predictor 学习率
   - --comp_lr (default=1e-3): CompensationHead 学习率
   - --sparsity_target (default=0.5)
   - 其他参数与 train.py 保持一致（model_name_or_path, num_steps, batch_size, seq_len, seed, gpu, output_dir, dataset, wandb_run_name 等）

   **Optimizer 设计**：
   - 两个 param groups: predictor params (predictor_lr) + comp_head params (comp_lr)
   - Phase 1 只有 predictor params 在 optimizer 中
   - Phase 2 添加 comp_head params（或初始化新 optimizer / 重建 param groups）

   **Forward pass（Phase 2）**：
   对每个 MLP 层：
   1. dense_output = MLP(x) — 完整前向
   2. sparse_mask = predictor(x) — 得到稀疏 mask
   3. sparse_output = dense_output * sparse_mask — 应用 mask
   4. comp_output = CompensationHead(sparse_output) — compensation
   5. comp_loss += MSE(comp_output, dense_output.detach())
   6. 最终 model output 用 comp_output 替代 sparse_output

   **Logging**：
   每 N 步打印：step, bce_loss, comp_loss, total_loss, sparsity, lr_pred, lr_comp, phase (warmup/joint)

   **Checkpoint**：
   保存时包含 predictor + comp_head 权重，文件名 predictor_bce_comp.pt

3. **文件注释**：
   - 文件顶部写文件说明（目的、两阶段流程、输入输出）
   - 关键函数写 docstring

4. **验证**：
   - `python src/train_bce_comp.py --help` 确认参数解析正确
   - 不要做 GPU dry-run（两张卡正在跑 kl_only 实验），只验证 import 和参数解析

### 服务器环境
- 环境激活：`source /root/distill_sparse_swiglu/setup_env.sh`
- 两张 GPU 正在训练，不要占用！

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# Loss functions: KL distillation, BCE activation prediction, compensation MSE, sparsity regularizer.\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\n\\n\\nclass KLDistillLoss(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"KL(P_dense || P_sparse) on output logit distributions.\\n\\n    P = softmax(logits / T). Scaled by T^2 so gradient magnitude is\\n    independent of temperature.\\n    Normalized to per-token-per-dim scale.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, temperature: float = 1.0):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.temperature = temperature\\n\\n    def forward(self, dense_logits: torch.Tensor, sparse_logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        p = F.softmax(dense_logits / self.temperature, dim=-1)\\n        log_q = F.log_softmax(sparse_logits / self.temperature, dim=-1)\\n        kl = F.kl_div(log_q, p, reduction=\\\"batchmean\\\") * (self.temperature ** 2)\\n        seq_len = dense_logits.shape[-2]\\n        return kl / seq_len\\n\\n\\nclass BCESparsityLoss(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"BCE between predictor logits and top-k binary activation targets.\\n\\n    For each position, the top (1-sparsity) fraction of neurons by absolute\\n    activation value are labeled 1 (keep); the rest 0 (prune).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, sparsity_target: float = 0.5):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.sparsity_target = sparsity_target\\n\\n    def forward(self, predictor_logits: torch.Tensor, dense_intermediate: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        k = int(dense_intermediate.shape[-1] * (1 - self.sparsity_target))\\n        abs_vals = dense_intermediate.abs()\\n        topk_vals, _ = abs_vals.topk(k, dim=-1)\\n        threshold = topk_vals[..., -1:]\\n        binary_target = (abs_vals >= threshold).float()\\n        return F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(predictor_logits, binary_target)\\n\\n\\nclass CompensationLoss(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"MSE between (sparse_mlp_output + compensation) and dense_mlp_output.\\n\\n    Operates per-layer in hidden_size space (after down_proj).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def forward(self, compensation: torch.Tensor, sparse_output: torch.Tensor,\\n                dense_output: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return F.mse_loss(sparse_output + compensation, dense_output)\\n\\n\\nclass SparsityRegularizer(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"L1 penalty driving average mask density toward (1 - target_sparsity).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, target_sparsity: float = 0.5, weight: float = 1.0):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.target_sparsity = target_sparsity\\n        self.weight = weight\\n\\n    def forward(self, masks: dict) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        if not masks:\\n            return torch.tensor(0.0)\\n        penalties = []\\n        for mask in masks.values():\\n            density = mask.mean()\\n            penalties.append(torch.abs(density - (1.0 - self.target_sparsity)))\\n        return self.weight * torch.stack(penalties).mean()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.52}}"}

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# Sparsity predictor architecture: bottleneck MLP + Gumbel-sigmoid mask + model wrapper.\\n# Compensation network: per-layer MLP correcting sparse MLP output errors (FastForward-style).\\n# Input: hidden_states (batch, seq, hidden_size) -> Output: mask logits (batch, seq, intermediate_size)\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\n\\n\\nclass SparsityPredictor(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Rank-bottleneck MLP predicting SwiGLU sparsity masks.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, intermediate_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 128):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.down = nn.Linear(hidden_size, bottleneck_size)\\n        self.act = nn.GELU()\\n        self.up = nn.Linear(bottleneck_size, intermediate_size)\\n\\n    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return self.up(self.act(self.down(hidden_states)))\\n\\n\\nclass CompensationHead(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Per-layer MLP predicting the output error introduced by sparsification.\\n\\n    Takes MLP input hidden state, outputs correction added to sparse MLP output.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 256):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_size, bottleneck_size),\\n            nn.GELU(),\\n            nn.Linear(bottleneck_size, hidden_size),\\n        )\\n\\n    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return self.net(hidden_states)\\n\\n\\nclass CompensationNetwork(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Per-layer compensation heads correcting sparse MLP output errors.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, num_layers: int, hidden_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 256):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.heads = nn.ModuleList([\\n            CompensationHead(hidden_size, bottleneck_size)\\n            for _ in range(num_layers)\\n        ])\\n\\n    def forward_layer(self, layer_idx: int, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return self.heads[layer_idx](hidden_states)\\n\\n\\nclass GumbelSigmoidMask(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Differentiable binary mask via Gumbel-sigmoid relaxation.\\n\\n    Training: soft mask with Gumbel noise and temperature annealing.\\n    Inference: hard threshold (sigmoid(logits) > 0.5 <=> logits > 0).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, tau: float = 1.0, hard: bool = False):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.tau = tau\\n        self.hard = hard\\n\\n    def forward(self, logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        if self.training and not self.hard:\\n            u = torch.rand_like(logits).clamp(1e-6, 1 - 1e-6)\\n            gumbel_noise = -torch.log(-torch.log(u))\\n            y_soft = torch.sigmoid((logits + gumbel_noise) / self.tau)\\n            return y_soft\\n        else:\\n            return (logits > 0).to(logits.dtype)\\n\\n\\nclass PredictorWrapper(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Wraps a frozen LlamaForCausalLM with per-layer sparsity predictors.\\n\\n    Monkey-patches each MLP to optionally apply a predicted mask on the\\n    SwiGLU intermediate activation (act(gate_proj(x)) * up_proj(x)) before down_proj.\\n\\n    Modes:\\n        sparse_mode=True:  apply Gumbel-sigmoid mask (training/eval)\\n        sparse_mode=False: passthrough; optionally capture intermediates for BCE\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, model, bottleneck_size: int = 128):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.model = model\\n        for p in model.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n\\n        config = model.config\\n        num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers\\n        hidden_size = config.hidden_size\\n        intermediate_size = config.intermediate_size\\n\\n        self.predictors = nn.ModuleList([\\n            SparsityPredictor(hidden_size, intermediate_size, bottleneck_size)\\n            for _ in range(num_layers)\\n        ])\\n        self.gumbel_mask = GumbelSigmoidMask()\\n\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\n        self.capture_intermediates = False\\n        self._layer_masks = {}\\n        self._layer_intermediates = {}\\n        self._layer_inputs = {}\\n\\n        # Compensation network (set externally, None by default)\\n        self.comp_network = None\\n        self.use_compensation = False\\n\\n        self._patch_mlps()\\n\\n    def _patch_mlps(self):\\n        for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(self.model.model.layers):\\n            self._patch_single_mlp(layer_idx, layer.mlp)\\n\\n    def _patch_single_mlp(self, layer_idx, mlp):\\n        gate_proj = mlp.gate_proj\\n        up_proj = mlp.up_proj\\n        down_proj = mlp.down_proj\\n        act_fn = mlp.act_fn\\n        wrapper = self\\n        predictor = self.predictors[layer_idx]\\n\\n        def patched_forward(x):\\n            gate = act_fn(gate_proj(x))\\n            up = up_proj(x)\\n            intermediate = gate * up\\n\\n            if wrapper.sparse_mode:\\n                logits = predictor(x)\\n                mask = wrapper.gumbel_mask(logits)\\n                wrapper._layer_masks[layer_idx] = mask\\n                result = down_proj(intermediate * mask)\\n                if wrapper.use_compensation and wrapper.comp_network is not None:\\n                    result = result + wrapper.comp_network.forward_layer(layer_idx, x)\\n                return result\\n            elif wrapper.capture_intermediates:\\n                wrapper._layer_intermediates[layer_idx] = intermediate.detach()\\n                wrapper._layer_inputs[layer_idx] = x.detach()\\n\\n            return down_proj(intermediate)\\n\\n        mlp.forward = patched_forward\\n\\n    def forward(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, labels=None):\\n        self._layer_masks.clear()\\n        return self.model(\\n            input_ids=input_ids,\\n            attention_mask=attention_mask,\\n            labels=labels,\\n        )\\n\\n    def forward_dense(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, capture_intermediates=False):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Forward without masks. Optionally captures per-layer intermediates & inputs.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.sparse_mode = False\\n        self.capture_intermediates = capture_intermediates\\n        self._layer_intermediates.clear()\\n        self._layer_inputs.clear()\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            outputs = self.model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\n        self.capture_intermediates = False\\n        return outputs\\n\\n    def forward_sparse(self, input_ids, attention_mask=None, labels=None):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Forward with predicted sparsity masks.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\n        return self.forward(input_ids, attention_mask, labels)\\n\\n    def get_masks(self):\\n        return dict(self._layer_masks)\\n\\n    def get_intermediates(self):\\n        return dict(self._layer_intermediates)\\n\\n    def get_layer_inputs(self):\\n        return dict(self._layer_inputs)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.53}}"}

correcting sparse MLP output errors (FastForward-style).\\n# Input: hidden_states (batch, seq, hidden_size) -> Output: mask logits (batch, seq, intermediate_size)\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\n\\n\\nclass SparsityPredictor(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Rank-bottleneck MLP predicting SwiGLU sparsity masks.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, intermediate_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 128):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.down = nn.Linear(hidden_size, bottleneck_size)\\n        self.act = nn.GELU()\\n        self.up = nn.Linear(bottleneck_size, intermediate_size)\\n\\n    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return self.up(self.act(self.down(hidden_states)))\\n\\n\\nclass CompensationHead(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Per-layer MLP predicting the output error introduced by sparsification.\\n\\n    Takes MLP input hidden state, outputs correction added to sparse MLP output.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 256):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_size, bottleneck_size),\\n            nn.GELU(),\\n            nn.Linear(bottleneck_size, hidden_size),\\n        )\\n\\n    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return self.net(hidden_states)\\n\\n\\nclass CompensationNetwork(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Per-layer compensation heads correcting sparse MLP output errors.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, num_layers: int, hidden_size: int, bottleneck_size: int = 256):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.heads = nn.ModuleList([\\n            CompensationHead(hidden_size, bottleneck_size)\\n            for _ in range(num_layers)\\n        ])\\n\\n    def forward_layer(self, layer_idx: int, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        return self.heads[layer_idx](hidden_states)\\n\\n\\nclass GumbelSigmoidMask(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Differentiable binary mask via Gumbel-sigmoid relaxation.\\n\\n    Training: soft mask with Gumbel noise and temperature annealing.\\n    Inference: hard threshold (sigmoid(logits) > 0.5 <=> logits > 0).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, tau: float = 1.0, hard: bool = False):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.tau = tau\\n        self.hard = hard\\n\\n    def forward(self, logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        if self.training and not self.hard:\\n            u = torch.rand_like(logits).clamp(1e-6, 1 - 1e-6)\\n            gumbel_noise = -torch.log(-torch.log(u))\\n            y_soft = torch.sigmoid((logits + gumbel_noise) / self.tau)\\n            return y_soft\\n        else:\\n            return (logits > 0).to(logits.dtype)\\n\\n\\nclass PredictorWrapper(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Wraps a frozen LlamaForCausalLM with per-layer sparsity predictors.\\n\\n    Monkey-patches each MLP to optionally apply a predicted mask on the\\n    SwiGLU intermediate activation (act(gate_proj(x)) * up_proj(x)) before down_proj.\\n\\n    Modes:\\n        sparse_mode=True:  apply Gumbel-sigmoid mask (training/eval)\\n        sparse_mode=False: passthrough; optionally capture intermediates for BCE\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, model, bottleneck_size: int = 128):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.model = model\\n        for p in model.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n\\n        config = model.config\\n        num_layers = config.num_hidden_layers\\n        hidden_size = config.hidden_size\\n        intermediate_size = config.intermediate_size\\n\\n        self.predictors = nn.ModuleList([\\n            SparsityPredictor(hidden_size, intermediate_size, bottleneck_size)\\n            for _ in range(num_layers)\\n        ])\\n        self.gumbel_mask = GumbelSigmoidMask()\\n\\n        self.sparse_mode = True\\n        self.capture_intermediates = False\\n        self._layer_masks = {}\\n        self._layer_intermediates = {}\\n        self._layer_inputs = {}\\n\\n        # Compensation network (set externally, None by default)\\n        self.comp_network = None\\n        self.use_compensation = False\\n\\n        self._patch_mlps()\\n\\n    def _patch_mlps(self):\\n        for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(self.model.model.layers):\\n            self._patch_single_mlp(layer_idx, layer.mlp)\\n\\n    def _patch_single_mlp(self, layer_idx, mlp):\\n        gate_proj = mlp.gate_proj\\n        up_proj = mlp.up_proj\\n        down_proj = mlp.down_proj\\n        act_fn = mlp.act_fn\\n        wrapper = self\\n        predictor = self.predictors[layer_idx]\\n\\n        def patched_forward(x):\\n            gate = act_fn(gate_proj(x))\\n torch.stack([m.mean() for m in masks.values()]).mean()\\n            sparsity_violation = target_sparsity - actual_sparsity\\n            constraint_loss = lambda_sparse * torch.clamp(sparsity_violation, min=0.0)\\n\\n            loss = (kl + constraint_loss) / ga\\n            loss.backward()\\n\\n            accum_loss += loss.item()\\n            accum_kl += kl.item() / ga\\n            accum_constraint += constraint_loss.item() / ga\\n\\n        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)\\n        optimizer.step()\\n        scheduler.step()\\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\\n\\n        masks = wrapper.get_masks()\\n        avg_sparsity = (\\n            1.0 - No further division.\\n    Soft constraint: clamp(|sparsity - target| - margin, 0)^2 for smooth gradients.\\n    Lambda: every-step update, x2.0 growth, x0.5 decay, cap=1000.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    kl_fn = KLDistillLoss(temperature=args.kl_temperature)\\n\\n    params = list(wrapper.predictors.parameters())\\n    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=args.lr, weight_decay=0.01)\\n    scheduler = cosine_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, args.warmup_steps, args.num_steps)\\n\\n    wrapper.predictors.train()\\n\\n    if args.gradient_checkpointing:\\n        wrapper.model.train()\\n        wrapper.model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

loss.backward()\\n\\n            accum_loss += loss.item()\\n            accum_bce += total_bce.item() / ga\\n            accum_reg += reg.item() / ga\\n\\n            last_sparsity = (\\n                1.0 - torch.stack([m.mean() for m in all_masks.values()]).mean().item()\\n                if all_masks else 0.0\\n            )\\n\\n        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)\\n        optimizer.step()\\n        scheduler.step()\\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\\n\\n        log_dict = {\\n            \\\"loss\\\": accum_loss, \\\"bce_loss\\\": accum_bce, \\\"reg_loss\\\": accum_reg,\\n            \\\"sparsity\\\": last_sparsity, \\\"lr\\\": scheduler.get_last_lr()[0],\\n        }\\n        wandb.log(log_dict, step=opt_step)\\n\\n        if opt_step % args.log_every == sequences.\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom torch.utils.data import IterableDataset, DataLoader\\nfrom datasets import load_dataset\\n\\n\\nclass PackedTokenDataset(IterableDataset):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Streams text, tokenizes, and packs into fixed-length (seq_len) chunks.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, hf_dataset, tokenizer, seq_len: int, seed: int = 42):\\n        self.dataset = hf_dataset\\n        self.tokenizer = tokenizer\\n        self.seq_len = seq_len\\n        self.seed = seed\\n\\n    def __iter__(self):\\n        buffer = []\\n        for example in self.dataset:\\n            text = example.get(\\\"text\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n            if not text.strip():\\n                continue\\n            tokens = self.tokenizer(text, add_special_tokens=False)[\\\"input_ids\\\"]\\n            buffer.extend(tokens)\\n            while len(buffer) >= self.seq_len + 1:\\n                chunk = buffer[: self.seq_len + 1]\\n                buffer = buffer[self.seq_len + 1 :]\\n                input_ids = torch.tensor(chunk[:-1], dtype=torch.long)\\n                labels = torch.tensor(chunk[1:], dtype=torch.long)\\n                yield {\\\"input_ids\\\": input_ids, \\\"labels\\\": labels}\\n\\n\\ndef get_calibration_loader(tokenizer, batch_size: int, seq_len: int, seed: int = 42,\\n                          dataset_name: str = \\\"wikitext-103\\\"):\\n    if dataset_name == \\\"wikitext-103\\\":\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"wikitext\\\", \\\"wikitext-103-raw-v1\\\", split=\\\"train\\\")\\n        ds = ds.shuffle(seed=seed)\\n    elif dataset_name == \\\"c4\\\":\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"allenai/c4\\\", \\\"en\\\", split=\\\"train\\\", streaming=True)\\n        ds = ds.shuffle(seed=seed, buffer_size=10000)\\n    else:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\\\")\\n    packed = PackedTokenDataset(ds, tokenizer, seq_len, seed)\\n    return DataLoader(packed, batch_size=batch_size, num_workers=0)\\n\\n\\n\\nclass RandomTokenDataset(IterableDataset):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Random token data for dry-run testing.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, vocab_size: int, seq_len: int, seed: int = 42):\\n        self.vocab_size = vocab_size\\n        self.seq_len = seq_len\\n        self.rng = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)\\n\\n    def __iter__(self):\\n        while True:\\n            tokens = torch.randint(0, self.vocab_size, (self.seq_len + 1,), generator=self.rng)\\n            yield {\\\"input_ids\\\": tokens[:-1], \\\"labels\\\": tokens[1:]}\\n\\n\\ndef get_random_loader(vocab_size: int, batch_size: int, seq_len: int, seed: int = 42):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Random DataLoader for dry-run testing without network access.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    ds = RandomTokenDataset(vocab_size, seq_len, evaluation dataset.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        name: \\\"wikitext2\\\" or \\\"c4\\\"\\n        tokenizer: HF tokenizer\\n        seq_len: target sequence length\\n        max_samples: cap on number of      ds = load_dataset(\\\"wikitext\\\", \\\"wikitext-2-raw-v1\\\", split=\\\"test\\\")\\n        texts = [ex[\\\"text\\\"] for ex in ds if ex[\\\"text\\\"].strip()]\\n    elif name == \\\"c4\\\":\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"allenai/c4\\\", \\\"en\\\", split=\\\"validation\\\", streaming=True)\\n        texts = []\\n        limit = (max_samples or 200) * 3\\n        for i, ex in examples = []\\n    for i in range(0, len(all_tokens) - seq_len, seq_len):\\n        chunk = all_tokens[i : i + seq_len + 1]\\n        if len(chunk) < seq_len + 1:\\n            break\\n        examples.append(\\n            {\\n                \\\"input_ids\\\": torch.tensor(chunk[:-1], dtype=torch.long),\\n                \\\"labels\\\": torch.tensor(chunk[1:], dtype=torch.long),\\n f\"loss={accum_loss:.4f} bce={accum_bce:.4f} comp={accum_comp:.4f} \"\n                f\"reg={accum_reg:.4f} sparsity={last_sparsity:.3f} \"\n                f\"lr_pred={optimizer.param_groups[0]['lr']:.2e} \"\n                f\"lr_comp={optimizer.param_groups[1]['lr']:.2e}\"\n            )\n\n\ndef main():\n    args = parse_args()\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\")\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    run_name = (args.wandb_run_name\n                or f\"bce_comp_s{args.sparsity_target}_plr{args.predictor_lr}_clr{args.comp_lr}\")\n    wandb.init(project=args.wandb_project, name=run_name, config=vars(args))\n\n    print(f\"Loading model from {args.model_name_or_path} ...\")\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_name_or_path)\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        args.model_name_or_path,\n        dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        device_map={\"\": device},\n        attn_implementation=_best_attn_impl(),\n    )\n    model.eval()\n\n    print(\"Initializing predictor wrapper ...\")\n    wrapper = PredictorWrapper(model, bottleneck_size=128)\n    wrapper.predictors.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n    num_layers = model.config.num_hidden_layers\n    hidden_size = model.config.hidden_size\n    comp_net = CompensationNetwork(num_layers, hidden_size, args.comp_bottleneck)\n    comp_net.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n    print(\"Setting up data loader ...\")\n    if args.use_random_data:\n        vocab_size = tokenizer.vocab_size\n        dataloader = get_random_loader(vocab_size, args.batch_size, args.seq_len, args.seed)\n        print(f\"Using random data (vocab_size={vocab_size})\")\n    else:\n        dataloader = get_calibration_loader(\n            tokenizer, args.batch_size, args.seq_len, args.seed, args.dataset\n        )\n\n    pred_params = sum(p.numel() for p in wrapper.predictors.parameters())\n    comp_params = sum(p.numel() for p in comp_net.parameters())\n    total_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())\n    print(f\"Predictor params: {pred_params:,} ({pred_params * 2 / 1024**2:.1f} MB bf16)\")\n    print(f\"Compensation params: {comp_params:,} ({comp_params * 2 / 1024**2:.1f} MB bf16)\")\n    print(f\"Model params: {total_params:,}\")\n    print(f\"Phase 1: BCE warmup for {args.comp_warmup_steps} steps\")\n    print(f\"Phase 2: BCE+Comp joint for {args.num_steps - args.comp_warmup_steps} steps\")\n\n    train(wrapper, comp_net, dataloader, args, device)\n\n p.add_argument(\"--seq_len\", type=int, default=2048)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    p.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", p.add_argument(\"--gradient_accumulation_steps\", type=int, default=1)\n    p.add_argument(\"--log_every\", type=int, default=10)\n    # LR schedule\n    p.add_argument(\"--predictor_lr\", type=float, p.add_argument(\"--comp_bottleneck\", type=int, default=256,\n                    help=\"CompensationHead bottleneck dimension\")\n    # W&B\n    p.add_argument(\"--wandb_project\", type=str, default=\"distill_sparse_swiglu\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--wandb_run_name\", type=str, default=None)\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\ndef _best_attn_impl():\n    if importlib.util.find_spec(\"flash_attn\"):\n        return \"flash_attention_2\"\n    return \"sdpa\"\n\n\ndef cosine_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, warmup_steps, total_steps):\n    def lr_lambda(step):\n        if step < warmup_steps:\n            return step / max(1, warmup_steps)\n        progress = (step - warmup_steps) / max(1, total_steps - warmup_steps)\n        return 0.5 * (1.0 + math.cos(math.pi * progress))\n    return torch.optim.lr_scheduler.LambdaLR(optimizer, lr_lambda)\n\n\ndef train(wrapper, comp_net, dataloader, args, device):\n    \"\"\"Two-phase training loop.\n\n    Phase 1 (step 1..comp_warmup_steps): BCE + SparsityReg, predictor only.\n    Phase 2 (step comp_warmup_steps+1..num_steps): BCE + CompMSE + SparsityReg,\n        predictor + CompensationHead jointly.\n    \"\"\"\n    bce_fn = BCESparsityLoss(sparsity_target=args.sparsity_target)\n    comp_loss_fn = CompensationLoss()\n    reg_fn = SparsityRegularizer(args.sparsity_target, args.sparsity_reg_weight)\n\n    # Freeze comp_net during Phase 1; optimizer holds both param groups but\n    # AdamW skips params with grad=None (requires_grad=False).\n    comp_net.requires_grad_(False)\n\n    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW([\n        {\"params\": list(wrapper.predictors.parameters()), \"lr\": args.predictor_lr},\n        {\"params\": list(comp_net.parameters()), \"lr\": args.comp_lr},\n    ], weight_decay=0.01)\n    scheduler = cosine_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, args.warmup_steps, args.num_steps)\n\n    wrapper.predictors.train()\n    comp_net.train()\n\n    data_iter = iter(dataloader)\n    ga = args.gradient_accumulation_steps\n    num_layers = len(wrapper.predictors)\n    phase2_started = False\n\n    for opt_step in range(1, args.num_steps + 1):\n        if opt_step == args.comp_warmup_steps + 1 and not phase2_started:\n            comp_net.requires_grad_(True)\n            phase2_started = True\n            print(f\"[Phase 2] Step {opt_step}: CompensationHead training enabled\")\n\n        tau = args.tau_start + (args.tau_end - args.tau_start) * (opt_step / args.num_steps)\n        wrapper.gumbel_mask.tau = tau\n\n        in_warmup = opt_step <= args.comp_warmup_steps\n\n        accum_loss = 0.0\n        accum_bce = 0.0\n        accum_comp = 0.0\n        accum_reg = 0.0\n        last_sparsity = 0.0\n\n        for _ in range(ga):\n            try:\n                batch = next(data_iter)\n            except StopIteration:\n                data_iter = iter(dataloader)\n                batch = next(data_iter)\n\n            input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n\n            wrapper.forward_dense(input_ids, capture_intermediates=True)\n            intermediates = wrapper.get_intermediates()\n            layer_inputs = wrapper.get_layer_inputs()\n\n            total_bce = torch.tensor(0.0, device=device)\n            total_comp = torch.tensor(0.0, device=device)\n            all_masks = {}\n            active_layers = 0\n\n            for layer_idx in range(num_layers):\n                if layer_idx not in intermediates:\n                    continue\n                active_layers += 1\n\n                dense_act = intermediates[layer_idx]\n                inp = layer_inputs[layer_idx]\n\n                pred_logits = wrapper.predictors[layer_idx](inp)\n                total_bce = total_bce + bce_fn(pred_logits, dense_act)\n\n                with torch.no_grad():\n                    mask = (pred_logits > 0).to(dense_act.dtype)\n                    all_masks[layer_idx] = mask\n\n                if not in_warmup:\n                    down_proj = wrapper.model.model.layers[layer_idx].mlp.down_proj\n                    with torch.no_grad():\n                        dense_out = down_proj(dense_act)\n                        sparse_out = down_proj(dense_act * mask)\n                    comp = comp_net.forward_layer(layer_idx, inp)\n                    total_comp = total_comp + comp_loss_fn(comp, sparse_out, dense_out)\n\n            total_bce = total_bce / max(active_layers, 1)\n            reg = reg_fn(all_masks)\n\n            if in_warmup:\n                loss = (total_bce + reg) / ga\n            else:\n                total_comp = total_comp / max(active_layers, 1)\n all_masks.values()]).mean().item()\n                if all_masks else 0.0\n            )\n\n        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(\n            list(wrapper.predictors.parameters()) + list(comp_net.parameters()), 1.0\n        )\n        optimizer.step()\n        scheduler.step()\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\n\n        phase_str = \"warmup\" if in_warmup else \"joint\"\n        log_dict = {\n            \"loss\": accum_loss, \"bce_loss\": accum_bce, \"comp_loss\": accum_comp,\n            \"reg_loss\": accum_reg, \"sparsity\": last_sparsity,\n            \"lr_pred\": optimizer.param_groups[0][\"lr\"],\n            \"lr_comp\": optimizer.param_groups[1][\"lr\"],\n            \"phase\": 1 if in_warmup else 2,\n        }\n        wandb.log(log_dict, {args.model_name_or_path} ...\")\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_name_or_path)\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        args.model_name_or_path,\n        dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        device_map={\"\": device},\n        attn_implementation=_best_attn_impl(),\n    num_layers = model.config.num_hidden_layers\n    hidden_size = model.config.hidden_size\n    comp_net = CompensationNetwork(num_layers, hidden_size, args.comp_bottleneck)\n 34	    )
35	    p.add_argument("--model_name_or_path", type=str,
36	                    default="/root/autodl-tmp/models/llama-3.1-8b")
37	    p.add_argument("--num_steps", type=int, default=1000)
38	    p.add_argument("--batch_size", type=int, default=4)
39	    p.add_argument("--seq_len", type=int, default=2048)
40	    p.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
41	    p.add_argument("--gpu", type=int, default=0)
42	    p.add_argument("--output_dir", type=str,
43	                    default="/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints")
44	    p.add_argument("--dataset", type=str, default="wikitext-103",
45	                    choices=["wikitext-103", "c4"])
46	    p.add_argument("--use_random_data", action="store_true",
47	                    help="Use random tokens instead of real data (for dry-run)")
48	    p.add_argument("--gradient_accumulation_steps", type=int, default=1)
49	    p.add_argument("--log_every", type=int, default=10)
50	    # LR schedule
51	    p.add_argument("--predictor_lr", type=float, default=1e-3)
52	    p.add_argument("--comp_lr", type=float, default=1e-3)
53	    p.add_argument("--warmup_steps", type=int, default=100,
54	                    help="LR cosine warmup steps")
55	    # Sparsity
56	    p.add_argument("--sparsity_target", type=float, default=0.5)
57	    p.add_argument("--sparsity_reg_weight", type=float, default=0.1)
58	    p.add_argument("--tau_start", type=float, default=1.0)
59	    p.add_argument("--tau_end", type=float, default=0.1)
60	    # Compensation
61	    p.add_argument("--comp_warmup_steps", type=int, default=200,
62	                    help="BCE-only warmup steps before enabling CompensationHead")
63	    p.add_argument("--comp_loss_weight", type=float, default=1.0,
64	                    help="Weight for compensation MSE loss in Phase 2")
65	    p.add_argument("--comp_bottleneck", type=int, default=256,
66	                    help="CompensationHead bottleneck dimension")
67	    # W&B
68	    p.add_argument("--wandb_project", type=str, default="distill_sparse_swiglu")
69	    p.add_argument("--wandb_run_name", type=str, default=None)
70	    return p.parse_args()
71	
72	
73	def _best_attn_impl():
74	    if importlib.util.find_spec("flash_attn"):
75	        return "flash_attention_2"
76	    return "sdpa"
77	
78	
79	def cosine_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, warmup_steps, total_steps):
80	    def lr_lambda(step):
81	        if step < warmup_steps:
82	            return step / max(1, warmup_steps)
83	        progress = (step - warmup_steps) / max(1, total_steps - warmup_steps)
84	        return 0.5 * (1.0 + math.cos(math.pi * progress))
85	    return torch.optim.lr_scheduler.LambdaLR(optimizer, lr_lambda)
86	
87	
88	def train(wrapper, comp_net, dataloader, args, device):
89	    """Two-phase training loop.
90	
91	    Phase 1 (step 1..comp_warmup_steps): BCE + SparsityReg, predictor only.
92	    Phase 2 (step comp_warmup_steps+1..num_steps): BCE + CompMSE + SparsityReg,
93	        predictor + CompensationHead jointly.
94	    """
95	    bce_fn = BCESparsityLoss(sparsity_target=args.sparsity_target)
96	    comp_loss_fn = CompensationLoss()
97	    reg_fn = SparsityRegularizer(args.sparsity_target, args.sparsity_reg_weight)
98	
99	    # Freeze comp_net during Phase 1; optimizer holds both param groups but
100	    # AdamW skips params with grad=None (requires_grad=False).
101	    comp_net.requires_grad_(False)
102	
103	    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW([
104	        {"params": list(wrapper.predictors.parameters()), "lr": args.predictor_lr},
105	        {"params": list(comp_net.parameters()), "lr": args.comp_lr},
106	    ], weight_decay=0.01)
107	    scheduler = cosine_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, args.warmup_steps, args.num_steps)
108	
109	    wrapper.predictors.train()
110	    comp_net.train()
111	
112	    data_iter = iter(dataloader)
113	    ga = args.gradient_accumulation_steps
114	    num_layers = len(wrapper.predictors)
115	    phase2_started = False
116	
117	    for opt_step in range(1, args.num_steps + 1):
118	        if opt_step == args.comp_warmup_steps + 1 and not phase2_started:
119	            comp_net.requires_grad_(True)
120	            phase2_started = True
121	            print(f"[Phase 2] Step {opt_step}: CompensationHead training enabled")
122	
123	        tau = args.tau_start + (args.tau_end - args.tau_start) * (opt_step / args.num_steps)
124	        wrapper.gumbel_mask.tau = tau
125	
126	        in_warmup = opt_step <= args.comp_warmup_steps
127	
128	        accum_loss = 0.0
129	        accum_bce = 0.0
130	        accum_comp = 0.0
131	        accum_reg = 0.0
132	        last_sparsity = 0.0
133	
134	        for _ in range(ga):
135	            try:
136	                batch = next(data_iter)
137	            except StopIteration:
138	                data_iter = iter(dataloader)
139	                batch = next(data_iter)
140	
141	            input_ids = batch["input_ids"].to(device)
142	
143	            wrapper.forward_dense(input_ids, capture_intermediates=True)
144	            intermediates = wrapper.get_intermediates()
145	            layer_inputs = wrapper.get_layer_inputs()
146	
147	            total_bce = torch.tensor(0.0, device=device)
148	            total_comp = torch.tensor(0.0, device=device)
149	            all_masks = {}
150	            active_layers = 0
151	
152	            for layer_idx in range(num_layers):
153	                if layer_idx not in intermediates:
154	                    continue
155	                active_layers += 1
156	
157	                dense_act = intermediates[layer_idx]
158	                inp = layer_inputs[layer_idx]
159	
160	                pred_logits = wrapper.predictors[layer_idx](inp)
161	                total_bce = total_bce + bce_fn(pred_logits, dense_act)
162	
163	                with torch.no_grad():
164	                    mask = (pred_logits > 0).to(dense_act.dtype)
165	                    all_masks[layer_idx] = mask
166	
167	                if not in_warmup:
168	                    down_proj = wrapper.model.model.layers[layer_idx].mlp.down_proj
169	                    with torch.no_grad():
170	                        dense_out = down_proj(dense_act)
171	                        sparse_out = down_proj(dense_act * mask)
172	                    comp = comp_net.forward_layer(layer_idx, inp)
173	                    total_comp = total_comp + comp_loss_fn(comp, sparse_out, dense_out)
174	
175	            total_bce = total_bce / max(active_layers, 1)
176	            reg = reg_fn(all_masks)
177	
178	            if in_warmup:
179	                loss = (total_bce + reg) / ga
180	            else:
181	                total_comp = total_comp / max(active_layers, 1)
182	                loss = (total_bce + args.comp_loss_weight * total_comp + reg) / ga
183	                accum_comp += total_comp.item() / ga
184	
185	            loss.backward()
186	            accum_loss += loss.item()
187	            accum_bce += total_bce.item() / ga
188	            accum_reg += reg.item() / ga
189	
190	            last_sparsity = (
191	                1.0 - torch.stack([m.mean() for m in all_masks.values()]).mean().item()
192	                if all_masks else 0.0
193	            )
194	
195	        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(
196	            list(wrapper.predictors.parameters()) + list(comp_net.parameters()), 1.0
197	        )
198	        optimizer.step()
199	        scheduler.step()
200	        optimizer.zero_grad()
201	
202	        phase_str = "warmup" if in_warmup else "joint"
203	        log_dict = {
204	            "loss": accum_loss, "bce_loss": accum_bce, "comp_loss": accum_comp,
205	            "reg_loss": accum_reg, "sparsity": last_sparsity,
206	            "lr_pred": optimizer.param_groups[0]["lr"],
207	            "lr_comp": optimizer.param_groups[1]["lr"],
208	            "phase": 1 if in_warmup else 2,
209	        }
210	        wandb.log(log_dict, step=opt_step)
211	
212	        if opt_step % args.log_every == 0 or opt_step == 1:
213	            print(
214	                f"[Step {opt_step}/{args.num_steps}] phase={phase_str} "
215	                f"loss={accum_loss:.4f} bce={accum_bce:.4f} comp={accum_comp:.4f} "
216	                f"reg={accum_reg:.4f} sparsity={last_sparsity:.3f} "
217	                f"lr_pred={optimizer.param_groups[0]['lr']:.2e} "
218	                f"lr_comp={optimizer.param_groups[1]['lr']:.2e}"
219	            )
220	
221	
222	def main():
223	    args = parse_args()
224	    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
225	    device = torch.device(f"cuda:{args.gpu}")
226	    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)
227	
228	    run_name = (args.wandb_run_name
229	                or f"bce_comp_s{args.sparsity_target}_plr{args.predictor_lr}_clr{args.comp_lr}")
230	    wandb.init(project=args.wandb_project, name=run_name, config=vars(args))
231	
232	    print(f"Loading model from {args.model_name_or_path} ...")
233	    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_name_or_path)
234	    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
235	        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token
236	
237	    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
238	        args.model_name_or_path,
239	        dtype=torch.bfloat16,
240	        device_map={"": device},
241	        attn_implementation=_best_attn_impl(),
242	    )
243	    model.eval()
244	
245	    print("Initializing predictor wrapper ...")
246	    wrapper = PredictorWrapper(model, bottleneck_size=128)
247	    wrapper.predictors.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)
248	
249	    num_layers = model.config.num_hidden_layers
250	    hidden_size = model.config.hidden_size
251	    comp_net = CompensationNetwork(num_layers, hidden_size, args.comp_bottleneck)
252	    comp_net.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)
253	
254	    print("Setting up data loader ...")
255	    if args.use_random_data:
256	        vocab_size = tokenizer.vocab_size
257	        dataloader = get_random_loader(vocab_size, args.batch_size, args.seq_len, args.seed)
258	        print(f"Using random data (vocab_size={vocab_size})")
259	    else:
260	        dataloader = get_calibration_loader(
261	            tokenizer, args.batch_size, args.seq_len, args.seed, args.dataset
262	        )
263	
264	    pred_params = sum(p.numel() for p in wrapper.predictors.parameters())
265	    comp_params = sum(p.numel() for p in comp_net.parameters())
266	    total_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())
267	    print(f"Predictor params: {pred_params:,} ({pred_params * 2 / 1024**2:.1f} MB bf16)")
268	    print(f"Compensation params: {comp_params:,} ({comp_params * 2 / 1024**2:.1f} MB bf16)")
269	    print(f"Model params: {total_params:,}")
270	    print(f"Phase 1: BCE warmup for {args.comp_warmup_steps} steps")
271	    print(f"Phase 2: BCE+Comp joint for {args.num_steps - args.comp_warmup_steps} steps")
272	
273	    train(wrapper, comp_net, dataloader, args, device)
274	
275	    ckpt_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, "predictor_bce_comp.pt")
276	    torch.save({
277	        "predictors": wrapper.predictors.state_dict(),
278	        "comp_network": comp_net.state_dict(),
279	        "args": vars(args),
280	    }, ckpt_path)
281	    print(f"Saved checkpoint -> {ckpt_path}")
282	
283	    wandb.finish()
284	
285	
286	if __name__ == "__main__":
287	    main()
288	
```

only with BCESparsityLoss + SparsityRegularizer.\n#   CompensationHead is frozen; no compensation loss.\n# Phase 2 (joint): train predictor + CompensationHead.\n#   Total loss = BCE + comp_loss_weight * CompensationMSE + reg_weight * SparsityReg.\n#\n# Input:  frozen Llama model + calibration data (wikitext-103 or c4)\n# Output: predictor_bce_comp.pt containing predictor + comp_head weights\n#\n# Usage:\n#   python train_bce_comp.py --num_steps 1000 --gpu 0\n#   python train_bce_comp.py --comp_warmup_steps 300 --predictor_lr 5e-4 --comp_lr 1e-3 --gpu 0\n\nimport argparse\nimport importlib.util\nimport math\nimport os\nimport sys\n\nimport torch\nimport wandb\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\n\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))\nfrom data_utils import get_calibration_loader, get_random_loader\nfrom losses import BCESparsityLoss, CompensationLoss, SparsityRegularizer\nfrom predictor import CompensationNetwork, PredictorWrapper\n\n\ndef parse_args():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Two-phase BCE+Compensation training for SwiGLU sparsity predictors\"\n    )\n    p.add_argument(\"--model_name_or_path\", type=str,\n                    default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/llama-3.1-8b\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--num_steps\", type=int, default=1000)\n    p.add_argument(\"--batch_size\", type=int, default=4)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seq_len\", type=int, default=2048)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    p.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str,\n                    default=\"/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--dataset\", type=str, default=\"wikitext-103\",\n                    choices=[\"wikitext-103\", \"c4\"])\n    p.add_argument(\"--use_random_data\", action=\"store_true\",\n                    help=\"Use random tokens instead of real data (for dry-run)\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--gradient_accumulation_steps\", type=int, default=1)\n    p.add_argument(\"--log_every\", type=int, default=10)\n    # LR schedule\n    p.add_argument(\"--predictor_lr\", type=float, default=1e-3)\n    p.add_argument(\"--comp_lr\", type=float, default=1e-3)\n    p.add_argument(\"--warmup_steps\", type=int, default=100,\n                    help=\"LR cosine warmup steps\")\n    # Sparsity\n    p.add_argument(\"--sparsity_target\", type=float, default=0.5)\n    p.add_argument(\"--sparsity_reg_weight\", type=float, default=0.1)\n    p.add_argument(\"--tau_start\", type=float, default=1.0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--tau_end\", type=float, default=0.1)\n    # Compensation\n    p.add_argument(\"--comp_warmup_steps\", type=int, default=200,\n                    help=\"BCE-only warmup steps before enabling CompensationHead\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--comp_loss_weight\", type=float, default=1.0,\n                    help=\"Weight for compensation MSE loss in Phase 2\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--comp_bottleneck\", type=int, default=256,\n                    help=\"CompensationHead bottleneck dimension\")\n    # W&B\n    p.add_argument(\"--wandb_project\", type=str, default=\"distill_sparse_swiglu\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--wandb_run_name\", type=str, default=None)\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\ndef _best_attn_impl():\n    if importlib.util.find_spec(\"flash_attn\"):\n        return \"flash_attention_2\"\n    return \"sdpa\"\n\n\ndef cosine_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, warmup_steps, total_steps):\n    def lr_lambda(step):\n        if step < warmup_steps:\n            return step / max(1, warmup_steps)\n        progress = (step - warmup_steps) / max(1, total_steps - warmup_steps)\n        return 0.5 * (1.0 + math.cos(math.pi * progress))\n    return torch.optim.lr_scheduler.LambdaLR(optimizer, lr_lambda)\n\n\ndef train(wrapper, comp_net, dataloader, args, device):\n    \"\"\"Two-phase training loop.\n\n    Phase 1 (step 1..comp_warmup_steps): BCE + SparsityReg, predictor only.\n    Phase 2 (step comp_warmup_steps+1..num_steps): BCE + CompMSE + SparsityReg,\n        predictor + CompensationHead jointly.\n    \"\"\"\n    bce_fn = BCESparsityLoss(sparsity_target=args.sparsity_target)\n    comp_loss_fn = CompensationLoss()\n    reg_fn = SparsityRegularizer(args.sparsity_target, args.sparsity_reg_weight)\n\n    # Freeze comp_net during Phase 1; optimizer holds both param groups but\n    # AdamW skips params with grad=None (requires_grad=False).\n    comp_net.requires_grad_(False)\n\n    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW([\n        {\"params\": list(wrapper.predictors.parameters()), \"lr\": args.predictor_lr},\n        {\"params\": list(comp_net.parameters()), \"lr\": args.comp_lr},\n    ], weight_decay=0.01)\n    scheduler = cosine_schedule_with_warmup(optimizer, args.warmup_steps, args.num_steps)\n\n    wrapper.predictors.train()\n    comp_net.train()\n\n    data_iter = iter(dataloader)\n    ga = args.gradient_accumulation_steps\n    num_layers = len(wrapper.predictors)\n    phase2_started = False\n\n    for opt_step in range(1, args.num_steps + 1):\n        if opt_step == args.comp_warmup_steps + 1 and not phase2_started:\n            comp_net.requires_grad_(True)\n            phase2_started = True\n            print(f\"[Phase 2] Step {opt_step}: CompensationHead training enabled\")\n\n        tau = args.tau_start + (args.tau_end - args.tau_start) * (opt_step / args.num_steps)\n        wrapper.gumbel_mask.tau = tau\n\n        in_warmup = opt_step <= args.comp_warmup_steps\n\n        accum_loss = 0.0\n        accum_bce = 0.0\n        accum_comp = 0.0\n        accum_reg = 0.0\n        last_sparsity = 0.0\n\n        for _ in range(ga):\n            try:\n                batch = next(data_iter)\n            except StopIteration:\n                data_iter = iter(dataloader)\n                batch = next(data_iter)\n\n            input_ids = batch[\"input_ids\"].to(device)\n\n            wrapper.forward_dense(input_ids, capture_intermediates=True)\n            intermediates = wrapper.get_intermediates()\n            layer_inputs = wrapper.get_layer_inputs()\n\n            total_bce = torch.tensor(0.0, device=device)\n            total_comp = torch.tensor(0.0, device=device)\n            all_masks = {}\n            active_layers = 0\n\n            for layer_idx in range(num_layers):\n                if layer_idx not in intermediates:\n                    continue\n                active_layers += 1\n\n                dense_act = intermediates[layer_idx]\n                inp = layer_inputs[layer_idx]\n\n                pred_logits = wrapper.predictors[layer_idx](inp)\n                total_bce = total_bce + bce_fn(pred_logits, dense_act)\n\n                with torch.no_grad():\n                    mask = (pred_logits > 0).to(dense_act.dtype)\n                    all_masks[layer_idx] = mask\n\n                if not in_warmup:\n                    down_proj = wrapper.model.model.layers[layer_idx].mlp.down_proj\n                    with torch.no_grad():\n                        dense_out = down_proj(dense_act)\n                        sparse_out = down_proj(dense_act * mask)\n                    comp = comp_net.forward_layer(layer_idx, inp)\n                    total_comp = total_comp + comp_loss_fn(comp, sparse_out, dense_out)\n\n            total_bce = total_bce / max(active_layers, 1)\n            reg = reg_fn(all_masks)\n\n            if in_warmup:\n                loss = (total_bce + reg) / ga\n            else:\n                total_comp = total_comp / max(active_layers, 1)\n                loss = (total_bce + args.comp_loss_weight * total_comp + reg) / ga\n                accum_comp += total_comp.item() / ga\n\n            loss.backward()\n            accum_loss += loss.item()\n            accum_bce += total_bce.item() / ga\n            accum_reg += reg.item() / ga\n\n            last_sparsity = (\n                1.0 - torch.stack([m.mean() for m in all_masks.values()]).mean().item()\n                if all_masks else 0.0\n            )\n\n        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(\n            list(wrapper.predictors.parameters()) + list(comp_net.parameters()), 1.0\n        )\n        optimizer.step()\n        scheduler.step()\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\n\n        phase_str = \"warmup\" if in_warmup else \"joint\"\n        log_dict = {\n            \"loss\": accum_loss, \"bce_loss\": accum_bce, \"comp_loss\": accum_comp,\n            \"reg_loss\": accum_reg, \"sparsity\": last_sparsity,\n            \"lr_pred\": optimizer.param_groups[0][\"lr\"],\n            \"lr_comp\": optimizer.param_groups[1][\"lr\"],\n            \"phase\": 1 if in_warmup else 2,\n        }\n        wandb.log(log_dict, step=opt_step)\n\n        if opt_step % args.log_every == 0 or opt_step == 1:\n            print(\n                f\"[Step {opt_step}/{args.num_steps}] phase={phase_str} \"\n                f\"loss={accum_loss:.4f} bce={accum_bce:.4f} comp={accum_comp:.4f} \"\n                f\"reg={accum_reg:.4f} sparsity={last_sparsity:.3f} \"\n                f\"lr_pred={optimizer.param_groups[0]['lr']:.2e} \"\n                f\"lr_comp={optimizer.param_groups[1]['lr']:.2e}\"\n            )\n\n\ndef main():\n    args = parse_args()\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\")\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    run_name = (args.wandb_run_name\n                or f\"bce_comp_s{args.sparsity_target}_plr{args.predictor_lr}_clr{args.comp_lr}\")\n    wandb.init(project=args.wandb_project, name=run_name, config=vars(args))\n\n    print(f\"Loading model from {args.model_name_or_path} ...\")\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_name_or_path)\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        args.model_name_or_path,\n        dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        device_map={\"\": device},\n        attn_implementation=_best_attn_impl(),\n    )\n    model.eval()\n\n    print(\"Initializing predictor wrapper ...\")\n    wrapper = PredictorWrapper(model, bottleneck_size=128)\n    wrapper.predictors.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n    num_layers = model.config.num_hidden_layers\n    hidden_size = model.config.hidden_size\n    comp_net = CompensationNetwork(num_layers, hidden_size, args.comp_bottleneck)\n    comp_net.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n    print(\"Setting up data loader ...\")\n    if args.use_random_data:\n        vocab_size = tokenizer.vocab_size\n        dataloader = get_random_loader(vocab_size, args.batch_size, args.seq_len, args.seed)\n        print(f\"Using random data (vocab_size={vocab_size})\")\n    else:\n        dataloader = get_calibration_loader(\n            tokenizer, args.batch_size, args.seq_len, args.seed, args.dataset\n        )\n\n    pred_params = sum(p.numel() for p in wrapper.predictors.parameters())\n    comp_params = sum(p.numel() for p in comp_net.parameters())\n    total_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())\n    print(f\"Predictor params: {pred_params:,} ({pred_params * 2 / 1024**2:.1f} MB bf16)\")\n    print(f\"Compensation params: {comp_params:,} ({comp_params * 2 / 1024**2:.1f} MB bf16)\")\n    print(f\"Model params: {total_params:,}\")\n    print(f\"Phase 1: BCE warmup for {args.comp_warmup_steps} steps\")\n    print(f\"Phase 2: BCE+Comp joint for {args.num_steps - args.comp_warmup_steps} steps\")\n\n    train(wrapper, comp_net, dataloader, args, device)\n\n    ckpt_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \"predictor_bce_comp.pt\")\n    torch.save({\n        \"predictors\": wrapper.predictors.state_dict(),\n        \"comp_network\": comp_net.state_dict(),\n ### Assistant

_source line 78 · 2026-05-15T17:35:33.753Z_

Import 和参数解析均通过。

`src/train_bce_comp.py` 已写入服务器，`--help` 验证通过。关键设计：

- **Phase 1** (step 1..`comp_warmup_steps`): BCE + SparsityReg，仅训练 predictor；CompensationHead 通过 `requires_grad_(False)` 冻结
- **Phase 2** (step `comp_warmup_steps+1`..`num_steps`): BCE + comp_loss_weight × CompMSE + SparsityReg，predictor + CompensationHead 联合训练
- **Optimizer**: 单个 AdamW 含两个 param group（predictor_lr / comp_lr），Phase 1 期间 comp params 无梯度自动跳过，Phase 2 解冻后无缝衔接
- **Checkpoint**: 保存 `predictor_bce_comp.pt`（predictors + comp_network + args）
